Projected gradient on simplex:
Initialize a uniform or parent normalized.
For each step:
  c = full conv(a,a) (using direct or FFT).
  Compute p=softmax(c/τ) (to avoid overflow). F not needed.
  grad = 2 * cross_corr(a,p).
  Update a ← project_simplex(a - lr*grad)

LP heuristic:
Given current a (sum1), M = max c (score/(2n)). For a subset of active lags I, solve primal:
maximize sum g, A_I g ≤ M, 0≤g≤U.
Then set g_norm = g / sum(g) (if finite, positive) and update a = (1-t)a + t*g_norm. If improves, accept.
Since sum1, M small ~1e-4. To scale for solver, multiply variables? Let h = S*g with S=1? We want b=M. If M 1e-4, A entries ~1/n~0.0003; b very small. Better scale by S=√(2n): Use aS = S*a (sum S). Mscore = score = 2n*M. LP: max sum h, A_s h ≤ Mscore? Where A_s rows use aS? (aS*h)_t ≤ Mscore? If h normalized to sum S. Then coefficients ~0.02, b~1.5. Good. Let f = S*a. M = score. U=b? Set u = S*U1 (e.g. 10). Solve max 1^T h s.t. A_h h ≤ M, 0≤h≤U. Use f coefficients. Then h_sum maybe >S. Rescale g = h / sum(h) to sum1. Mix.

LP with active rows: For each row t, A[t,j]=f_{t-j} if 0≤t-j<n. We can build dense A for I. If len I <= maxK (e.g., 600). Use scipy.optimize.linprog(c=-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(M), bounds=(0,U), method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasible'?}). If solve success and h finite and >0.

Now, full sequence MSA details.

n=3809, sum1. a positive. Let loga.

Function score_and_corr(loga or a):
- a=exp(loga - max)/sum; normalize maybe store.
- fa=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]; if tiny negative clip? for max use c.max().
- score=2n*c.max()
- For gradient: given c/temperature, p=softmax. If use top q, p can be one-hot. Use v=2*corr1.
- corr1=irfft(fa*conj(fp),N)[:n] real.
Alternatively for single lag k, v_i=2*a[indices]. Need indexing. If k in 0..L-1: valid j? v_i = 2 a_{k-i} for i where 0≤k-i≤n-1. Let i_start=max(0,k-(n-1)), i_end=min(n-1,k). def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p

conv_fft:
```
def conv_fft(a, N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:a.size*2-1]
```

Normalize function from loga:
```
def normalize_log(loga):
    m=loga.max()
    e=np.exp(loga-m)
    e/=e.sum()
    # loga = np.log(e) ? this is costly. Or loga = loga - (m+log(e.sum()))
    sc = m + np.log(e.sum())
    loga -= sc
    return loga
```

Let's implement exact_score_array:
```
def exact_score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return float(2*a.size*max(c)/s*s)
```
For internal score with sum1, can use fft_score.

FFT score:
```
def fft_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return inf
    a=a/s
    c=conv_fft(a,N)
    return 2*a.size*float(np.max(c))
```
But conv_fft assumes a sum not used. If a sum1, score=2nmax. Good.

def largest_remainder(n, frac):
    frac=np.asarray(frac,float); frac/=frac.sum()
    raw=np.floor(n*frac)
    rem=n-raw.sum()
    if rem<=0: adjust? if rem<0, subtract from largest raw.
    sort = np.argsort(n*frac - raw)[::-1]
    raw[sort[:rem]] +=1
    # ensure at least 1? if raw zero for a block, maybe okay B reduces. Better enforce min_frac? Could allow some zero; heights for zero not used. But B effective smaller. To avoid division by zero, if any 0, set to 1 and reduce from largest. Simpler: if raw has 0, we can increase one of largest? Use `raw = np.maximum(raw,1);` then fix sum by subtracting from largest until sum n.
```
Implement robust:
```
def lengths_from_frac(n, frac):
    frac=np.clip(frac,1e-12,None); frac/=frac.sum()
    raw=np.floor(n*frac).astype(int)
    if raw.min()<1:
       # assign at least 1 to each block: if B>n return None
       raw=np.ones(B,int)
    while raw.sum()>n:
       idx=np.argmax(raw)
       if raw[idx]<=1: break
       raw[idx]-=1
    while raw.sum()<n:
       # give to fractional remainders
       rem=(n*frac)-raw
       idx=np.argmax(rem)
       raw[idx]+=1
    return raw
```

def optimize_stream(a0, t_start, t_end, rng, config):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0)
    best_local=a.copy(); best_local_score=fft_score(a) # maybe exact less often
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    c=conv_fft(a); cmax=c.max()
    if cmax<=0: return
    # top list refresh
    fail_count=0
    step=0
    batch=config.batch
    base_alphas=np.array([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.25,0.6,1.2])
    while time.monotonic()<t_end:
        if step % 20 ==0:
           # refresh c, accept local if better
           c=conv_fft(a); score=2*n*c.max()
           if score < best_local_score:
               best_local_score=score; best_local=a.copy()
               exact=exact_score_array(a); if exact<state best...
           # if no local improvement for fail_evals and time, perturb/reset
        # choose lags from c
        if c.max()>0:
            top_k = min(64,L)
            # To avoid argmax only, use weights from c sorted
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
            w = c[idx]
            if w.sum()>0: w=w/w.sum()
        # sample batch idxs
        sel = np.random.choice(idx, size=batch, replace=False, p=w)
        v=np.zeros(n)
        for k in sel:
             k=int(k)
             jlo=max(0,k-n+1); jhi=min(n-1,k)
             if jhi>=jlo:
                v[jlo:jhi+1] += (2.0/batch) * a[k-jhi : k-jlo+1][::-1]
        maxv=v.max()
        if maxv <= 1e-300:
             # add noise
        else:
             best_a_step=None; best_score_step=fft_score(a)
             for alpha in base_alphas:
                 step=alpha/maxv
                 u=loga - step*v
                 m=u.max(); e=np.exp(u-m); e/=e.sum()
                 cand=e
                 # maybe exact? fft
                 s_ft=fft_score(cand)
                 if s_ft < best_score_step - 1e-12:
                     best_score_step=s_ft; best_a_step=cand
             if best_a_step is not None:
                 a=best_a_step; loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); fail_count=0
             else:
                 fail_count+=1
                 # random noise or try large alpha
                 if fail_count > 20:
                     # add noise to loga: loga += sigma normal; normalize; if score not drastically worse accept else reset to best_local
                     sigma = 0.1*cmax? log space? z in loga relative to log(1/n) ~ -7.2. Use sigma 0.1.
                     loga += rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
                     normalize log -> a
                     fail_count=0
                     if time%50? evaluate
                 else:
                     # maybe choose a smaller alpha always? If no improvement, try alpha=0.005. Or sample different rag (stale c).
        step+=1
```

def consider(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if len(a)==0 or not finite: return
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01 return
    # normalize? score scale invariant but we can leave. To keep evaluator cap, fine.
    sc=exact_score_array(a)
    if finite and sc < state['best_score']:
        state['best_arr']=a.copy()/s? maybe leave; if we normalize to sum1, score same and small. Use normalized for return? Evaluator clips; no problem. Return list of normalized or original? Both. Let's return sum1 to avoid large. state best normalized sum1.
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline=start+budget-15
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # get parent
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0 and finite all and p.sum()>0: parent=clip
    except: pass
    if parent is None: n=3809; parent=np.ones(n)/n
    else: n=parent.size
    n=int(parent.size)
    if n<2: n=3809...
    # maybe if n too large for fft? if n>20000 choose? parent 3809. If no parent, n=3809.
    parent_norm=parent/parent.sum()
    state={'best':parent_norm.copy(), 'score':exact_score_array(parent_norm)}
    # FFT N
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    # define consider with state,N,n
    # initial candidates
    init_pool=[parent_norm, np.ones(n)/n]
    if n<=20000: add block/black? 
    # block simple two grid and optimizer
    deadline_block = min(hard_deadline, start+max(20,0.15*budget))
    run_block_search(n, state, deadline_block, rng)
    # MSA
    deadline_msa = min(hard_deadline - 20, start+0.55*budget) maybe
    configs = [ {'batch':1,...} ...]
    starts = [state.best.copy(), parent_norm.copy(), ones/n, maybe random smooth]
    for run...
    # LP if time remaining >120
    deadline_lp = max(deadline_msa+? , hard_deadline-20)
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline-80:
       run_lp(state, deadline_lp)
    # final validation
    final=state.best.copy(); final=clip; if sum<0.01 fallback
    sc=exact_score_array(final)
    if not finite: fallback
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```

Implement `score_fft(a)`:
```
def score_fft(a, N):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01 return inf
   a=a/s
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N); cmax=float(np.max(c[:2*a.size-1]))
   if cmax<0: cmax=0
   return 2*a.size*cmax  # since sum1
```
For non-sum1, if normalize; score invariant.

`pattern_score(x, n_opt, B)`:
  split lengths u_len=x[:B-1], masses u_mass=x[B-1:]
  len_frac=softmax(np.concatenate([u_len, [0.0]]))
  mass_frac=softmax(np.concatenate([u_mass, [0.0]]))
  lengths = lengths_from_frac(n_opt, len_frac, B)
  if lengths is None: return 1e9
  a=np.empty(n_opt)
  s=0; start=0
  for i,L in enumerate(lengths):
      h=mass_frac[i]/L
      a[start:start+L]=h
      start+=L
  a/=a.sum()
  return float(np.max(conv_fft(a,Nopt))*2*n_opt)
```
Nopt precomputed.

Now, random smooth inits. Function `random_smooth(n, rng, seed)`:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
a=np.ones(n)
for _ in range(3):
   f=rng.uniform(1,6)
   amp=rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)
   phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   a *= np.exp(amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x+phase))
a *= np.exp(rng.uniform(-1,1)*(x-0.5)*2)
return normalize(a)
```
Also one-sided exponential: a=exp(β x). Provide few.

LP implementation code:
```
def lp_improve(a0, deadline, rng, state, N, n):
    try:
       import scipy.optimize as so
    except: return
    S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    f=a0*S
    c=conv_fft(f,N)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite or M<=0: return
    L=2*n-1
    K0=16
    top=np.argpartition(c, -K0)[-K0:]
    even=np.linspace(0,L-1,K0).astype(int)
    I=np.unique(np.concatenate([top,even,[n-1,0,L-1]]))
    U=max(S/200, 5*S/n)
    maxK=80 if n>2000 else 200
    max_rounds=4
    for round in range(max_rounds):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline-30: break
       K=len(I)
       if K==0 or K>maxK: break
       A=np.zeros((K,n), float)
       for r,t in enumerate(I): ... fill
       try:
          res=so.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(K,M), bounds=(0.0,U), method='highs')
       except Exception:
          try method='interior-point'? no; break
       if res.status not in (0,3?) and not res.success: continue
       g=res.x
       if g is None or not finite or g.sum()<=1e-8*S: continue
       v=conv_fft(g? f*g not f*f)
```

`_conv_fft`:
```
def _conv_fft(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    m=a.size*2-1
    return c[:m]
```
Could produce small imaginary? irfft real.

`_conv2_fft`:
```
def _conv2_fft(a,b,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:2*a.size-1] # assume same length
```

`_score_fft(a,N)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)

if not finite or s<0.01: return inf
a=a/s
c=_conv_fft(a,N)
cm=float(np.max(c))
if not finite or cm<0: cm=0
return float(2*a.size*cm)
```

a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01 return inf
c=np.convolve(a,a)
return float(2*a.size*max(0,float(np.max(c)))/(s*s))
```

Now, block pattern factory using Nopt.

```
def _make_block_objective(n_opt,Nopt,B):
    def f(u):
       if u.size != 2*(B-1): return 1e9
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)): return 1e9
       ul=u[:B-1]; um=u[B-1:]
       len_frac=_softmax(np.concatenate([ul,[0.0]]))
       mass_frac=_softmax(np.concatenate([um,[0.0]]))
       Ls=_largest_remainder(n_opt,len_frac,B)
       if Ls is None: return 1e9
       a=np.empty(n_opt)
       pos=0
       for i,Li in enumerate(Ls):
           if Li<=0: continue
           a[pos:pos+Li]=mass_frac[i]/Li
           pos+=Li
       if pos<n_opt: # adjust? should not
           a[pos:]=a[-1]
       a/=a.sum()
       return _score_fft(a,Nopt)
    return f
```
`_largest_remainder` returns array length B of positive ints summing n, or None if B>n.
Implement:
```
def _largest_remainder(n, frac, B):
   B=int(B)
   if B>n: return None
   frac=np.asarray(frac,float); frac=np.clip(frac,1e-12,None)
   frac=frac/frac.sum()
   raw=np.floor(n*frac).astype(int)
   # ensure at least 1
   raw=np.maximum(raw,1)
   if raw.size!=B: raw=np.resize(raw,B)
   # adjust sum
   # If raw sum > n due forcing ones, cap: if B==n, all ones. Else reduce largest until sum n.
   # But forcing ones may increase sum if floor was 0. We'll do iterative.
   order=np.argsort(n*frac - raw)[::-1] # remainders before forcing? maybe
   # We need sum n. Set raw = np.zeros; give one to each: raw=np.ones(B)
   # then distribute n-B by remainders.
   raw=np.ones(B,int)
   rem=n-B
   fracs = frac
   # sort by fractional part of n*frac?
   while rem>0:
       # pick max (n*frac_i - raw_i)
       delta = n*fracs - raw
       idx=int(np.argmax(delta))
       raw[idx]+=1
       rem-=1
       # to avoid infinite if rem huge? n up to 3809, loop 3809 could be okay but many calls? Too slow.
```
Need efficient. Standard largest remainder: raw_floor=floor(n*frac); remainder = n - sum raw_floor. If some raw_floor=0, we can first allocate floor then distribute remainder to top fractional parts. If raw_floor has zero, it may get 1 if high frac. But need positive. We can allow zero blocks? For pattern, if a block length zero, reduce B effectively; objective still works if we handle division by zero by skipping? Better allow zero: If length 0, set height 0, but mass for that block should be reallocated? mass_frac has mass for zero block; if skip, sum<1. We can instead if any length 0, add 1 to it by removing from largest (if n>=B). So enforce positive.

Efficient positive lengths:
```
raw = np.maximum(np.floor(n*frac), 1.0).astype(int)
total = raw.sum()
if total > n:
   # reduce from largest, but keep >=1
   # amount = total-n
   order = np.argsort(raw)[::-1]
   red = total-n
   for idx in order:
       if red<=0: break
       if raw[idx] <=1: continue
       take = min(raw[idx]-1, red)
       raw[idx]-=take; red-=take
   if red>0: return None # B too large? but B<=n
else:
   # need add n-total to largest remainders of n*frac - raw
   rem = n-total
   fr = n*frac - raw
   order = np.argsort(fr)[::-1]
   # If rem maybe large, iterating rem up to n can be okay per objective? n_opt=512, rem<=512, 1200*512 loops ~600k fine. For target n=3809 rem up to 3809, but only few target generations.
   for idx in order:
       if rem<=0: break
       raw[idx]+=1; rem-=1
   if rem>0:
       # distribute cyclically
       for idx in range(order.size):
          if rem<=0: break
          raw[idx]+=1; rem-=1
return raw
```
The iterative add for rem may add one by one; for n=3809 and B=8, up to 3801 loops once, fine. For proxy many, 512 loops*1200=600k, okay. Could vectorize but fine.

But if raw after max floor sum > n by more than reducible? If B close n; okay.

Block search:
```
def _block_search(state,N,n,rng,deadline,n_opt=512):
    Nopt=_next_pow2(2*n_opt-1)
    try: import scipy.optimize as opt
    except: return
    best_x={}
    max_B=8
    B_list=[2,3,4,5,6,8]
    reps=2
    for B in B_list:
      if time>deadline: break
      for rep in range(reps):
        x0=rng.normal(size=2*(B-1))*0.5
        f=_make_block_objective(n_opt,Nopt,B)
        try:
            res=opt.minimize(f,x0,method='Powell',options={'maxiter':60,'maxfev':200})
            x=res.x if res.success or res.fun<f(x0) else x0
        except: continue
        a=_make_from_pattern(x,n,B)
        _try_update(state,a,N)
```

Detailed MSA code:
```
def _run_msa(a0, deadline, rng, state, N, n, config):
    a=np.asarray(a0,float).copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return
    a=a/s
    # if a score much worse than state best? Start anyway.
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    alphas=np.array(config.get('alphas',[0.02,0.1,0.5,2.0]))
    batch=int(config.get('batch',3))
    topm=int(config.get('top',32))
    fail=0; step=0
    local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=_score_fft(a,N)
    tmp=np.empty(n); e=np.empty(n)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       step+=1
       c=_conv_fft(a,N)
       cm=float(np.max(c))
       if not finite or cm<=0: break
       base_score=2*n*cm
       if base_score < local_best_score - 1e-12:
            local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=base_score
            if base_score < state['best_fft'] - 1e-8:
                 _try_update(state,a,N)
       # top lags
       if c.size <= topm:
           top=np.arange(c.size)
       else:
           top=np.argpartition(c, -topm)[-topm:]
       w=c[top]
       if w.sum() <= 0: w=np.ones_like(w)
       w=w/w.sum()
       # sample
       bsz=min(batch, top.size)
       sel=rng.choice(top, size=bsz, replace=False, p=w)
       v=np.zeros(n)
       for kk in sel:
           k=int(kk)
           jlo=k-(n-1)
           if jlo<0: jlo=0
           jhi=k
           if jhi>=n: jhi=n-1
           if jhi>=jlo:
              # coeff = 2/bsz * a[k-jhi : k-jlo+1][::-1]
              seg=a[k-jhi : k-jlo+1][::-1]
              v[jlo:jhi+1] += (2.0/bsz)*seg
       mv=float(v.max())
       if mv <= 1e-300:
           # random noise
       else:
           best_a=None; best_s=base_score
           # maybe skip beta if alphas too large? 
           for alpha in alphas:
               if alpha<=0: continue
               gam=alpha/mv
               # u = loga - gam*v
               np.multiply(v, gam, out=tmp)
               u = loga - tmp  # alloc
               m=u.max()
               np.exp(u-m, out=e)
               ss=e.sum()
               if ss<=0 or not finite: continue
               e /= ss
               sc=_score_fft(e,N)
               if finite and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s=sc; best_a=e.copy()
           if best_a is not None:
               a=best_a; loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); fail=0
               local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=best_s
               if best_s < state['best_fft'] - 1e-8:
                    _try_update(state,a,N)
           else:
               fail+=1
               if fail > 25:
                  # restart from local_best with noise
                  noise_scale = 0.1
                  loga_noise = np.log(np.maximum(local_best,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n)
                  m=loga_noise.max(); e=np.exp(loga_noise-m); e/=e.sum()
                  a=e; loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
                  sc=_score_fft(a,N)
                  if sc < local_best_score * 1.2:
                      local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=sc
                  fail=0
               else:
                  # try a smaller random alpha not in list
                  alpha=rng.uniform(0.005,0.2)
                  ... candidate; if improves accept else fail
    # after loop, maybe update state
    if local_best_score < state['best_fft'] - 1e-9:
         _try_update(state,local_best,N)
```
Memory: `u=loga - tmp` allocates; e alloc. good.

But note `tmp` reused, after `u=loga-tmp`, tmp unchanged. For next alpha, overwritten. Good.

But if high-frequency noise, convolution maybe? Jump not regular. Now, LP code implement with N. Need ensure if n large, K max small. Use `N` precomputed. Function:
```
def _lp_heuristic(a0, deadline, rng, state, N, n):
    try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except: return
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    f=a0*S
    c=_conv_fft(f,N)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite or M<=0: return
    L=2*n-1
    topm=12
    top=np.argpartition(c,-min(topm,L))[-min(topm,L):]
    even=np.linspace(0,L-1,12).astype(int)
    extra=np.array([0,L-1,n-1])
    I=np.unique(np.concatenate([top,even,extra]))
    maxK=60 if n>2000 else 200
    addC=16
    U=max(S/200, 5*S/n)
    round=0
    while round<3 and time.monotonic()<deadline-30:
       round+=1
       K=len(I)
       if K==0 or K>maxK: break
       A=np.zeros((K,n),float)
       for r,t in enumerate(I):
          jlo=max(0,t-(n-1)); jhi=min(n-1,t)
          if jhi>=jlo:
             A[r,jlo:jhi+1]=f[t-jhi:t-jlo+1][::-1]
       try:
          cvec=-np.ones(n)
          res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(K,M), bounds=(0.0,U), method='highs',
                      options={'presolve': True, 'output_flag': False})
       except Exception as ex:
          # fallback to smaller K? break
          break
       if res.x is None: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       if not finite or g.sum()<=0: break
       # compute f*g
       fg=_conv2_fft(f,g,N)
       viol=np.max(fg)-M
       if viol > M*2:
           # too infeasible, add more constraints if possible
           if len(I) < maxK:
               pos=np.max(fg-M)
               # top viol
               vviol=fg-M
               add=np.argpartition(vviol, -min(addC,L))[-min(addC,L):]
               I=np.unique(np.concatenate([I,add]))
               if I.size > maxK: I=I[:maxK]
               continue
           else: break
       # feasible or mild, use direction
       gsum=float(np.sum(g))
       if gsum<=1e-12: break
       dirs=[]
       d1=g/gsum; d1=np.clip(d1,0,None); d1=d1/d1.sum()
       # denoised
       d2=d1*0.5 + (S/n)*0.5; d2/=d2.sum()
       # clip top
       d3=np.minimum(d1, 0.05); d3=np.clip(d3,0,None)
       if d3.sum()>0: d3/=d3.sum(); dirs.append(d3)
       dirs.append(d1); dirs.append(d2)
       if viol>0 and len(I)<maxK:
           # add violated and maybe continue another round? But use direction now too.
           vviol=fg-M
           add=np.argpartition(vviol, -min(addC,L))[-min(addC,L):]
           I=np.unique(np.concatenate([I,add]))
           if I.size>maxK: I=I[:maxK]
       for d in dirs:
           for t in (0.25,0.5,0.75):
              cand=(1-t)*a0 + t*d
              cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum()
              if s>0:
                 cand=cand/s
                 _try_update(state,cand,N)
       # If round done, maybe break if feasible
       if viol <= 1e-6*max(1,M): break
    # end
```
This may be okay. However, `_try_update` inside LP calls exact O(n^2) potentially 3*3=9 times, fine.

Cross_corr uses FFT. In MSA, we already compute c. For LSE mode:
```
z=c/tau; p=exp(z-z.max()); p/=p.sum()

`_block_search`:
```
def _block_search(state,N,n,rng,deadline):
  n_opt=min(n,1024); Nopt=...
  try import scipy.optimize
  except: return
  B_list=[2,3,4,5,6,8]
  # random samples
  for B in B_list:
    obj=_make_block_objective(n_opt,Nopt,B)
    best_x=None; best=inf
    for _ in range(20):
       x=rng.normal(0,0.5,size=2*(B-1))
       val=obj(x)
       if val<best: best=val; best_x=x.copy()
    if best_x is None: continue
    a=_make_from_pattern(best_x,n,B); _try_update(state,a,N,force=True)
  # Powell for B 2,3,4,5
  for B in [2,3,4,5]:
    if time>deadline: break
    for _ in range(1):
       x0=rng.normal(0,0.5,size=2*(B-1))
       obj=...
       try: res=opt.minimize(obj,x0,method='Powell',options={'maxiter':50,'maxfev':150})
             x=res.x
       except: continue
       a=...; update
```
Maybe use random best_x as start for Powell to improve. Good.

In `_make_block_objective`, using `_score_fft(a,Nopt)` exact score sum normalized. It doesn't account for score scaling with n_opt; we optimize pattern for proxy score (2n_opt max). ```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size)
    return e / s


def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < max(1, int(m)):
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return None
    return a / s


def _conv_fft(a, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:2 * a.size - 1]


def _conv2_fft(a, b, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    out_len = 2 * a.size - 1
    if b.size != a.size:
        # Fallback for safety; not expected in this program.
        return np.convolve(a, b)[:out_len]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:out_len]


def _score_fft(a, N):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_fft(a, N)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm < 0.0:
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2 * a.size * cm)


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm < 0.0:
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2 * a.size * cm / (s * s))


def _try_update(state, a, N, force=False):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return False
    scft = _score_fft(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(scft):
        return False
    if not force and scft > state.get("best_fft", float("inf")) - 1e-8:
        return False
    ex = _exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(ex):
        return False
    if ex < state.get("best_exact", float("inf")) - 1e-12:
        state["best"] = a.copy()
        state["best_exact"] = float(ex)
        state["best_fft"] = float(scft)
        return True
    return False


def _largest_remainder(n, frac, B):
    B = int(B)
    n = int(n)
    if B <= 0 or B > n:
        return None
    frac = np.asarray(frac, dtype=float).ravel()[:B]
    if frac.size != B:
        frac = np.pad(frac, (0, B - frac.size))
    frac = np.clip(frac, 1e-15, None)
    s = frac.sum()
    if s <= 0.0:
        frac = np.full(B, 1.0 / B)
    else:
        frac = frac / s

    raw = np.floor(n * frac).astype(int)
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 1)

    total = int(raw.sum())
    if total > n:
        order = np.argsort(raw)[::-1]
        extra = total - n
        for idx in order:
            if extra <= 0:
                break
            if raw[idx] <= 1:
                continue
            take = min(int(raw[idx] - 1), extra)
            raw[idx] -= take
            extra -= take
        if extra > 0:
            return None
    else:
        rem = n - total
        if rem > 0:
            delta = n * frac - raw
            order = np.argsort(delta, kind="stable")[::-1]
            # One-by-one is small enough for the proxy sizes used here.
            idx = 0
            while rem > 0:
                raw[order[idx % B]] += 1
                rem -= 1
                idx += 1
                if idx > 4 * n + B and rem > 0:
                    break
    return raw.astype(int)


def _make_block_objective(n_opt, N_opt, B, deadline=None):
    def f(u):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            return 1e12
        u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float).ravel()
        need = 2 * (B - 1)
        if u.size != need:
            return 1e12
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
            return 1e12
        ul = u[:B - 1]
        um = u[B - 1:]
        len_frac = _softmax(np.concatenate((ul, np.array([0.0]))))
        mass_frac = _softmax(np.concatenate((um, np.array([0.0]))))
        Ls = _largest_remainder(n_opt, len_frac, B)
        if Ls is None:
            return 1e12
        a = np.zeros(n_opt, dtype=float)
        pos = 0
        for i, Li in enumerate(Ls):
            if Li <= 0:
                continue
            a[pos:pos + Li] = mass_frac[i] / float(Li)
            pos += Li
        if pos < n_opt:
            a[pos:] = float(a[pos - 1]) if pos > 0 else 1.0
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a is None:
            return 1e12
        return _score_fft(a, N_opt)
    return f


def _make_from_pattern(x, n, B):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    need = 2 * (B - 1)
    if x.size != need:
        x = np.pad(x, (0, need - x.size)) if x.size < need else x[:need]
    ul = x[:B - 1]
    um = x[B - 1:]
    len_frac = _softmax(np.concatenate((ul, np.array([0.0]))))
    mass_frac = _softmax(np.concatenate((um, np.array([0.0]))))
    Ls = _largest_remainder(n, len_frac, B)
    if Ls is None:
        return None
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    pos = 0
    for i, Li in enumerate(Ls):
        if Li <= 0:
            continue
        a[pos:pos + Li] = mass_frac[i] / float(Li)
        pos += Li
    if pos < n and pos > 0:
        a[pos:] = float(a[pos - 1])
    return _normalize(a)


def _block_search(state, N, n, rng, deadline):
    n_opt = int(min(n, 1024))
    if n_opt < 2:
        n_opt = 2
    N_opt = _next_pow2(2 * n_opt - 1)
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
    except Exception:
        _minimize = None

    B_list = [2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8]
    random_samples = 18 if n >= 1000 else 8

    for B in B_list:
        if B > n_opt:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        obj = _make_block_objective(n_opt, N_opt, B, deadline)
        best_x = None
        best_val = float("inf")
        for _ in range(random_samples):
            x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=2 * (B - 1))
            try:
                v = float(obj(x))
            except Exception:
                v = 1e12
            if np.isfinite(v) and v < best_val:
                best_val = v
                best_x = x.copy()
        if best_x is None:
            continue
        a = _make_from_pattern(best_x, n, B)
        if a is not None:
            _try_update(state, a, N, force=True)

    if _minimize is None:
        return

    powB = [2, 3, 4, 5]
    for B in powB:
        if B > n_opt or time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        obj = _make_block_objective(n_opt, N_opt, B, deadline)
        x0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=2 * (B - 1))
        try:
            res = _minimize(obj, x0, method="Powell",
                            options={"maxiter": 45, "maxfev": 140})
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float) if res is not None else x0
        except Exception:
            x = x0
        a = _make_from_pattern(x, n, B)
        if a is not None:
            _try_update(state, a, N, force=True)


def _random_smooth(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(3):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.25, 1.75)
        freq = rng.uniform(1.0, 6.0)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        a *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x + phase))
    slope = rng.uniform(-2.2, 2.2)
    a *= np.exp(slope * (x - 0.5) * 2.0)
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    return a


def _run_msa(a0, deadline, rng, state, N, n, config):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    if a is None:
        a0f = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).ravel()
        if a0f.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a0f)):
            return
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    else:
        a = a.astype(float, copy=True)

    alphas = np.asarray(config.get("alphas", [0.02, 0.1, 0.5, 2.0]), dtype=float)
    batch = int(config.get("batch", 3))
    topm = int(config.get("top", 32))
    lsep = config.get("lsep", None)
    tiny = 1e-300

    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny))
    local_best = a.copy()
    local_best_score = _score_fft(a, N)
    fail = 0
    tmp = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    e = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    v = np.empty(n, dtype=float)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _conv_fft(a, N)
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
            break
        base_score = float(2 * n * cm)
        if base_score < local_best_score - 1e-12:
            local_best = a.copy()
            local_best_score = float(base_score)
            if base_score < state.get("best_fft", float("inf")) - 1e-8:
                _try_update(state, a, N, force=False)

        if lsep is not None:
            tau = max(1e-300, cm / float(lsep))
            z = c / tau
            z -= float(np.max(z))
            p = np.exp(z)
            ps = float(p.sum())
            if ps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
                fail += 1
                break
            p /= ps
            fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
            fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
            corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fp, N)[:n]
            np.multiply(corr, 2.0, out=v)
        else:
            if c.size <= max(1, topm):
                top = np.arange(c.size, dtype=int)
            else:
                top = np.argpartition(c, -topm)[-topm:].astype(int)
            w = c[top].astype(float)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.full(top.size, 1.0 / max(1, top.size), dtype=float)
            else:
                w /= ws
                w = np.maximum(w, 1e-300)
                w /= w.sum()
            bsz = min(batch, top.size)
            if bsz <= 0:
                fail += 1
                break
            sel = rng.choice(top, size=bsz, replace=False, p=w)
            v.fill(0.0)
            for kk in sel:
                k = int(kk)
                jlo = k - (n - 1)
                if jlo < 0:
                    jlo = 0
                jhi = k
                if jhi >= n:
                    jhi = n - 1
                if jhi >= jlo:
                    seg = a[k - jhi:k - jlo + 1][::-1]
                    v[jlo:jhi + 1] += (2.0 / float(bsz)) * seg

        mv = float(np.max(v))
        if not np.isfinite(mv) or mv <= 1e-300:
            fail += 1
        else:
            best_a = None
            best_s = base_score
            for alpha in alphas:
                alpha = float(alpha)
                if not np.isfinite(alpha) or alpha <= 0.0:
                    continue
                gam = alpha / mv
                np.multiply(v, gam, out=tmp)
                u = loga - tmp
                m = float(np.max(u))
                if not np.isfinite(m):
                    continue
                np.exp(u - m, out=e)
                ss = float(e.sum())
                if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
                    continue
                e /= ss
                sc = _score_fft(e, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s = float(sc)
                    best_a = e.copy()

            if best_a is not None:
                a = best_a
                loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny))
                local_best = a.copy()
                local_best_score = float(best_s)
                fail = 0
                if best_s < state.get("best_fft", float("inf")) - 1e-7:
                    _try_update(state, a, N, force=False)
                elif fail > 200 and fail % 50 == 0:
                    _try_update(state, a, N, force=False)
            else:
                fail += 1
                if fail > 25:
                    lb = _normalize(local_best)
                    if lb is None:
                        lb = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
                    scale = 0.12
                    noise_log = np.log(np.maximum(lb, tiny)) + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
                    m = float(np.max(noise_log))
                    np.exp(noise_log - m, out=e)
                    ss = float(e.sum())
                    if ss > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ss):
                        e /= ss
                        sc = _score_fft(e, N)
                        a = e
                        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny))
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_score * 1.25 + 1e-12:
                            local_best = a.copy()
                            local_best_score = float(sc)
                    fail = 0
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        pass
    _try_update(state, local_best, N, force=(local_best_score < state.get("best_fft", float("inf")) + 1e-8))


def _lp_heuristic(a0, deadline, rng, state, N, n):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
        return
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
        return

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    f = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float) * S
    c = _conv_fft(f, N)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return
    L = 2 * n - 1

    topm = 12
    if L <= topm:
        top = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    else:
        top = np.argpartition(c, -topm)[-topm:].astype(int)
    even = np.linspace(0, L - 1, 12, dtype=int)
    extra = np.array([0, L - 1, n - 1, n // 2], dtype=int)
    I = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, even, extra))).astype(int)

    maxK = 60 if n > 2000 else 180
    addC = 18
    U = max(S / 200.0, 5.0 * S / n)
    one = np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    for _round in range(3):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40.0:
            break
        if I.size == 0 or I.size > maxK:
            break
        K = int(I.size)
        A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=float)
        for r, t in enumerate(I):
            t = int(t)
            jlo = max(0, t - (n - 1))
            jhi = min(n - 1, t)
            if jhi >= jlo:
                A[r, jlo:jhi + 1] = f[t - jhi:t - jlo + 1][::-1]
        try:
            res = linprog(-one,
                          A_ub=A,
                          b_ub=np.full(K, float(M)),
                          bounds=(0.0, float(U)),
                          method="highs",
                          options={"presolve": True, "output_flag": False})
        except Exception:
            break
        g = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if g is None:
            break
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            break

        fg = _conv2_fft(f, g, N)
        viol = float(np.max(fg) - M)

        if viol > M * 2.0:
            if I.size < maxK:
                vv = fg - M
                addm = min(addC, L)
                if L <= addm:
                    add = np.argsort(vv)[::-1][:addm]
                else:
                    add = np.argpartition(vv, -addm)[-addm:]
                I = np.unique(np.concatenate((I, add))).astype(int)
                if I.size > maxK:
                    I = I[:maxK]
            continue

        d1 = g / gs
        d1 = _normalize(d1)
        dirs = []
        if d1 is not None:
            dirs.append(d1)
            d2 = _normalize(0.5 * d1 + 0.5 * (S / n))
            if d2 is not None:
                dirs.append(d2)
            d3 = np.minimum(d1, 0.05)
            d3 = _normalize(d3)
            if d3 is not None:
                dirs.append(d3)

        for d in dirs:
            for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                cand = (1.0 - t) * np.asarray(a0, dtype=float) + t * d
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                if cand is not None:
                    _try_update(state, cand, N, force=True)

        if viol <= max(1.0, M) * 1e-6:
            break
        if I.size < maxK:
            vv = fg - M
            addm = min(addC, L)
            if L <= addm:
                add = np.argsort(vv)[::-1][:addm]
            else:
                add = np.argpartition(vv, -addm)[-addm:]
            I = np.unique(np.concatenate((I, add))).astype(int)
            if I.size > maxK:
                I = I[:maxK]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.5, float(budget_s))
    if budget > 30.0:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 20.0)
    else:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.2, budget - max(0.2, 0.2 * budget))
    if hard_deadline <= t0 + 0.2:
        hard_deadline = t0 + 0.2

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n > 20000:
            n = 3809
            parent = None
        else:
            p = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
            p = p[np.isfinite(p)]
            if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = p / float(p.sum())
            else:
                parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.size < 2:
        n = 3809
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    else:
        parent = parent.astype(float, copy=True)

    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    state = {
        "best": parent.copy(),
        "best_exact": float("inf"),
        "best_fft": float("inf"),
    }
    _try_update(state, parent, N, force=True)

    if state["best_exact"] == float("inf"):
        uni = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
        _try_update(state, uni, N, force=True)

    if int(budget) > 80 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 60.0:
        block_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 120.0, t0 + min(120.0, 0.10 * budget))
        _block_search(state, N, n, rng, block_deadline)

    for _ in range(4):
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 100.0:
            break
        a = _random_smooth(n, rng)
        _try_update(state, a, N, force=True)

    msa_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 90.0, t0 + min(900.0, 0.62 * budget))
    if time.monotonic() < msa_deadline - 10.0:
        configs = [
            {"batch": 1, "top": 16, "alphas": [0.05, 0.25, 1.0]},
            {"batch": 12, "top": 48, "alphas": [0.02, 0.1, 0.5]},
            {"lsep": 5.0, "alphas": [0.02, 0.1, 0.5]},
            {"batch": 1, "top": 8, "alphas": [0.1, 0.5, 2.0]},
        ]
        remaining = max(0.0, msa_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if remaining <= 20.0:
            nruns = 1
        else:
            nruns = min(len(configs), max(1, int(math.ceil(remaining / 120.0))))
        seg = remaining / max(1, nruns)
        for ci in range(nruns):
            end = min(msa_deadline, time.monotonic() + seg)
            if end <= time.monotonic() + 2.0:
                break
            start_arr = state["best"].copy()
            try:
                _run_msa(start_arr, end, rng, state, N, n, configs[ci])
            except Exception:
                break
        # If substantial time remains, run another stochastic pass on the current best.
        if time.monotonic() < msa_deadline - 60.0:
            try:
                _run_msa(state["best"].copy(), msa_deadline, rng, state, N, n,
                         {"batch": 2, "top": 24, "alphas": [0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 3.0]})
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 70.0:
        try:
            _lp_heuristic(state["best"].copy(), hard_deadline - 8.0, rng, state, N, n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    candidates = [state.get("best"), parent, np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n]
    final_arr = None
    final_score = float("inf")
    for arr in candidates:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        arr = _normalize(arr)
        if arr is None:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr]))
        except Exception:
            sc = _exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = sc
            final_arr = arr.copy()

    if final_arr is None:
        final_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n

    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        final_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    else:
        final_arr /= s

    return [float(x) for x in final_arr]
```
